### [1] [Vibe Coding: Practice, Performance, Productivity, and Risk -A State-of-the-Art Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20446)
*Dominik L. Michels,Mutaz Abu Ghazaleh,Francois Lazzari,Nabil Kassem,Jonathan Klein*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该摘要是一篇关于“氛围编程”（vibe coding）的跨学科证据综述，核心结论是：AI辅助开发在生成新代码时可能带来效率收益，但其效果高度依赖任务、测量口径、代码库成熟度与观察周期；在缺陷检测、文档可审计性、安全性、代码质量和开发者技能保持方面存在显著风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在整合软件工程、人机交互、劳动经济学、安全、治理与教育等领域的早期实证研究，以解释AI辅助开发生产率结论相互矛盾的原因，并评估其能力边界和潜在代价。摘要指出，现有讨论常将代码产出、主观感受和真实生产率混为一谈，因而需要系统梳理不同研究设计下的证据。

Method: 作者采用最先进综述方法，汇集跨学科语料，覆盖模型能力、工具生态、按任务类型划分的性能表现，以及现场实验、随机试验和团队遥测等不同证据来源。分析框架重点控制测量方法、研究范围和时间跨度，并归纳造成结果离散的六类模式，包括测量范围扩大后的效应收缩、自我报告与独立测量不一致、将产出量误作生产率，以及长期检验导致早期强结论回调等。

Result: 摘要报告：在部分同行评议现场实验中，任务周完成量提高26%；独立随机试验却观察到19%的速度下降；团队遥测数据显示代码评审时间增加441%。作者认为这些结果并非必然冲突，而是反映了研究设计与任务环境差异。能力方面，代码生成相对可靠，但故障检测较弱、文档难以审计。风险方面，已部署应用出现安全缺陷，大规模代码与开发者遥测显示代码质量下降，版权责任尚未厘清，并有开发技能退化的证据。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将表面矛盾的生产率研究置于统一解释框架：AI辅助开发的收益不能仅凭短期、局部或自报指标判断。作者提出可证伪猜想：该类工具对新代码开发存在真实增益，但在成熟代码库中的收益会缩小甚至转为负值；若被后续研究证实，该机制可解释当前大部分经验结果分歧。

Abstract: Vibe coding - AI-assisted software development in which the developer describes intent in natural language and validates results by running rather than reading the generated code - was named by Andrej Karpathy in February 2025 and produced its first body of empirical evidence within seventeen months. This state-of-the-art review assembles that evidence across a cross-disciplinary corpus spanning software engineering, human-computer interaction, labour economics, security research, governance, and education. We survey the model landscape, the tool ecosystem, and the performance record by task type, finding the early benchmarks saturated but task-level capability uneven: reliable code generation alongside weak fault detection and hard-to-audit documentation. The productivity record is at first contradictory: peer-reviewed field experiments report +26% more tasks per week, independent randomised trials measure a 19% slowdown, and team-level telemetry shows code-review time up +441%. We argue these readings are consistent once measurement method, scope, and time horizon are held constant, and identify six patterns behind the dispersion, among them effect-shrinkage under broader measurement, self-report diverging from independent measurement, output volume conflated with productivity, and bold claims walked back once tested over longer horizons. We further document security failures in deployed applications, code-quality degradation visible in large-scale code and developer telemetry, unsettled copyright exposure, and evidence of skill atrophy. The review closes with the open research questions and one falsifiable conjecture: that the gains are real on new code and shrink or reverse on mature codebases, which would account for most of the disagreement in the record.

</details>


### [2] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Code Translation Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20776)
*Ruihang Fan,Jiajun Jiang,Xinpeng Wang,Jiateng Fu,Fengjie Li,Jiasi Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模实证比较学习式、LLM式及通用LLM代码翻译技术，发现LLM相关方法在方法级可执行正确性上总体更优，但类别级翻译仍受全局语义与跨方法依赖制约，且代码相似度不能替代功能正确性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动代码翻译研究缺少对不同技术范式、不同翻译粒度及不同语言方向的系统性比较，导致各方法的适用边界、性能差异和主要失效原因尚不明确。

Method: 研究在多语言的方法级与类别级基准上，评估代表性学习式方法、LLM式方法和通用LLM；采用可执行正确性与代码相似度等指标，并从翻译方向、粒度差异及失败模式等维度开展对比分析。

Result: LLM及LLM式方法在方法级功能正确性方面通常优于学习式方法；相似度指标与实际功能正确性并不稳定一致。翻译方向会显著影响结果，尤其是涉及类型系统特征差异较大的语言对时。类别级任务明显更难，主要因为需同时维护全局语义、接口、成员关系与跨方法依赖。主要错误类型为静态语义错误和逻辑错误。

Conclusion: 代码翻译系统的评估应以可执行功能正确性为核心，而非仅依赖文本或结构相似度。后续方法应增强类型感知、程序结构感知和上下文感知能力，并重点处理跨方法依赖及静态语义约束，以提升复杂粒度翻译的可靠性。

Abstract: Automated code translation is increasingly important for software evolution, yet the relative strengths and limitations of learning-based and large language model (LLM)-based techniques remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study comparing representative code translation techniques across methodological paradigms and translation granularities. We evaluate learning-based methods, LLM-based methods, and general-purpose LLMs on multilingual method-level and class-level benchmarks involving multiple programming languages. Our analysis considers executable correctness, code similarity, translation direction, translation granularity, and failure patterns. The results show that LLMs and LLM-based methods generally outperform learning-based methods in method-level correctness, although similarity metrics alone do not reliably reflect functional correctness. Translation direction substantially affects performance, particularly when translating between languages with different type-system characteristics. Class-level translation remains considerably more difficult than method-level translation because it requires preserving global semantics, interfaces, member relationships, and cross-method dependencies. Our error analysis further shows that static semantic errors and logical errors are the primary challenges in existing code translation systems. These findings provide empirical evidence and practical guidance for developing more robust, type-aware, structure-aware, and context-aware code translation techniques.

</details>


### [3] [Making Deployments Safe at Meta: Health Checks for Continuous Change-Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20513)
*Prakash KL,Anton Korenkov,Uttam Thakore,Christopher Hegre*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文介绍了Meta用于大规模持续部署的Service Health Checker（SHC）：在发布过程中自动评估服务健康状态，发现回归后触发自动回滚，以平衡发布速度与系统可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生产环境中的每次变更都可能引入可靠性回归，而延迟发布又会损失业务与工程效率。异构服务数量庞大时，依赖人工监控和决策难以规模化，因此需要统一、自动化且可配置的发布期健康检查机制。

Method: SHC采用预防式分布式架构。检查作者通过模板化指标查询、阈值及工作流谓词定义健康检查规则；系统与分层发布、分阶段灰度发布流程集成，在发布各阶段持续评估指标，并在检测到异常回归时执行自动回滚。论文还讨论了配套的度量体系、工具改进和默认配置优化。

Result: 该基础设施可覆盖数千个异构服务，将健康验证嵌入部署工作流，实现发布期的自动化风险拦截与回滚。长期运行中，系统暴露出指标噪声、告警疲劳、规则与服务行为漂移以及未被覆盖的回归等问题；作者通过测量、工具和更优默认策略对这些问题进行治理。

Conclusion: 部署健康检查是连接持续交付效率与可靠性保障的关键基础设施。其有效性不仅取决于规则表达和自动回滚能力，还依赖对噪声、漂移、覆盖率及运维负担的持续治理。未来方向是利用AI辅助进行健康检查调优，提高规则配置和异常识别的适应性。

Abstract: Continuous deployment to large scale production systems creates a tension between release velocity and reliability. Every change is a potential reliability incident, yet every delay is a missed opportunity. This paper describes the deployment time health check infrastructure that Meta uses to mediate this tension across thousands of heterogeneous services. We summarize the architecture of this prevention based distributed system's service called Service Health Checker, explain how check authors compose templated metric queries, thresholds, and workflow predicates; and discuss how the system is integrated with tiered and phased rollouts so that regressions trigger automatic rollback. We then describe the operational problems that emerged at scale, such as noise, alert fatigue, drift, and uncovered regressions, and the program of measurement, tooling, and improved defaults we deployed to address them. We close with lessons learned from years of operating deployment health checks at Meta, and the directions we are exploring next, including AI assisted health check tuning. Index Terms: deployment safety, continuous deployment, monitoring, software reliability, release engineering, software reliability engineering, AIOps, anomaly detection

</details>


### [4] [Testing and Evaluation of Agentic AI Systems In Military Command and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20597)
*Ulysse Richard,Heather Frase,Sarah Cao,Di Cooke,Sebastian Kwon,Adrianna Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are being procured for military command and control (C2) under public commitments to rigorous testing and human oversight. Whether such commitments can be discharged depends on their supporting assurance case, which requires three elements: claims specifying the conditions for acceptability, evidence bearing on those claims, and an argument connecting the two. Through a structured review of 240 documented Testing and Evaluation (T&E) practices, spanning eight evaluation dimensions and three lifecycle stages, we identify eight assumptions that established methods make about their test article, grouped into four clusters: system specifiability, stability, composability, and supervisability. Agentic properties weaken all eight assumptions. This erosion affects the argument connecting evidence to claims, not the claims or evidence themselves. As a result, test results may satisfy process requirements, but they do not warrant the inference from tested to fielded behavior.
  We derive ten assurance claims for the first three assumption clusters and assess whether current and emerging methods can address each, mapping operational consequences through five C2 scenarios. Supervisability is identified but not assessed here, since evidencing it depends on system stability results and human factors T&E methods beyond the present scope. The documented record does not support broad claims about system-level behavior, but narrower claims remain recoverable in principle, contingent on mature methods: bounded mission envelopes, trajectory-grounded correctness, executable runtime constraints, and characterized run-to-run variance. Part of the evidentiary burden shifts into deployment, making the determination to field a continuing act. Where evidence cannot be generated, the residual uncertainty can be governed through defined expiry conditions and assigned ownership.

</details>


### [5] [Behavior Specification-Guided Program Synthesis for Binary Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20628)
*Kangchen Zhu,Shangwen Wang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhouyang Jia,Xiaoling Li,Jun Ma,Jie Yu,Xiaoguang Mao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BinMirror将二进制反混淆重新定义为行为规范引导的程序合成问题，通过动态执行轨迹和交互快照生成并验证源代码，在极端混淆条件下取得74.5%的单元测试Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制反混淆通常依赖反编译和结构变换，但编译过程会丢失类型信息及源代码结构，导致生成代码质量较低，且难以保证与原程序运行行为一致。

Method: BinMirror以二进制程序的动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规范，合成具有高可读性的源代码，并通过与混淆二进制采集的运行时观测结果进行验证，从结构恢复转向行为驱动的程序合成。

Result: 在包含150万个合成混淆二进制的实验中，BinMirror显著优于现有先进基线；在极端混淆场景下，单元测试Pass@1达到74.5%。

Conclusion: 行为规范引导的合成范式能够有效绕过二进制反编译中的语义缺失问题，在保持运行行为一致性的同时恢复更具语义清晰度和实用价值的源代码，适用于实际安全分析。

Abstract: Deobfuscation is critical to reverse engineering and security analysis because it restores the readability and analyzability of obfuscated code. However, existing research primarily focuses on source-code deobfuscation, while binary-level deobfuscation remains largely underexplored despite its practical importance when source code is unavailable. Existing binary deobfuscation methods typically decompile binaries into pseudocode and then apply structural transformations. However, because compilation discards high-level semantics such as precise type information and source-level structures, this decompilation-based paradigm often produces low-quality code and provides limited assurance that the recovered code preserves the runtime behavior of the original program. To address these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift from structural transformation to behavior-driven synthesis. Our core insight is that although obfuscation distorts a program's internal structure, semantics-preserving transformations must retain its observable execution behavior. Based on this insight, we introduce BinMirror, an approach that reformulates binary deobfuscation as a behavior-specification-guided program synthesis task. By treating dynamic execution traces and interaction snapshots as behavioral specifications, BinMirror synthesizes high-quality source code and validates it against runtime observations collected from heavily obfuscated binaries. Extensive evaluations on 1.5 million synthetically obfuscated binaries show that BinMirror significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a unit-test Pass@1 of 74.5% under extreme obfuscation. These results demonstrate the practical utility of BinMirror in restoring semantic clarity for real-world security analysis.

</details>


### [6] [Specification Portability Across LLM Development Agents: Cross-Agent Compatibility in Specification-Driven Software Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21208)
*Oleg Grynets,Oleksii Ilchuk,Dariia Zatulna,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文以Oracle迁移至PostgreSQL为受控任务，研究软件规格在不同智能体之间的可移植性。结论表明，规格并非天然“智能体中立”：跨智能体直接复用可能显著降低生成质量；改写与检索增强通常更有效，但效果依赖具体智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规格驱动开发（SDD）常假设规格可被不同开发智能体一致理解和执行，但这一假设缺少实证验证。本文旨在量化规格来源、表达方式及传递策略对跨智能体代码生成与数据库迁移质量的影响。

Method: 研究分两阶段进行：第一阶段在1,006个PL/SQL文件上评估规格优先的迁移流水线，统计代码再生成及PostgreSQL 16执行成功情况；第二阶段基于1,802组Oracle脚本及其PostgreSQL对应实现，测试Kiro、Gemini、Copilot等智能体生成或消费原生与外来规格的表现。评估指标包括Token F1、完全匹配、SQL语法有效性、AST完全匹配、AST平均相似度和即时可运行性，并比较直接摄取、改写、压缩及检索增强等策略。

Result: 第一阶段中，623/1,006个PL/SQL文件成功再生成，380个生成脚本可在PostgreSQL 16中成功执行。第二阶段显示，规格长度不能预测实现质量，跨智能体传递存在明显且依赖智能体的性能退化。最强复现实例为Gemini直接使用Kiro生成的规格：Token F1为0.035，SQL语法有效率为2.33%，AST平均相似度为0.015。针对Gemini的测试中，规格改写显著改善结果；压缩没有普遍收益；检索增强是Gemini和Copilot各自Pareto前沿中唯一共同出现的策略。

Conclusion: 异构SDD工作流不应默认将规格视为可无损跨智能体迁移的通用工件。工程实践应显式评估规格可移植性，针对目标智能体调整规格表达，并优先考虑通过检索增强方式提供上下文与规格访问。

Abstract: This paper investigates cross-agent specification portability using Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration as a controlled software transformation task. The study combines two experimental stages. First, a specification-first migration pipeline was evaluated on 1,006 PL/SQL files, of which 623 were successfully regenerated and 380 generated scripts executed successfully in PostgreSQL 16. Second, cross-agent experiments were conducted on a dataset of 1,802 Oracle scripts with corresponding PostgreSQL implementations using Amazon Kiro, Google Gemini, and GitHub Copilot, with Claude Code and Cursor included in the initial single-agent evaluation. Native and foreign specifications were assessed using Token F1, exact match, SQL syntax validity, AST exact match, AST mean similarity, and immediate runnability. The results show that specification size alone does not predict implementation quality and that cross-agent transfer can produce substantial agent-dependent degradation. The strongest replicated case occurred when Gemini directly consumed a Kiro-origin specification, producing a Token F1 of 0.035, SQL syntax validity of 2.33%, and AST mean similarity of 0.015. Rewriting substantially improved Gemini in the tested configuration, compression did not provide a universal benefit, and retrieval-augmented ingestion was the only common strategy represented on the per-agent Pareto frontiers of both Gemini and Copilot. The findings suggest that specifications in heterogeneous SDD workflows should not automatically be treated as agent-neutral artifacts and motivate explicit consideration of specification portability, agent-specific interpretation, and retrieval-based access in multi-agent software engineering.

</details>


### [7] [Toward Understanding Operating System Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20643)
*Hongyao Zuo,Jiali Li,Jiajun Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Android、Linux和HarmonyOS中共1,500个缺陷进行大规模实证研究，从多维度刻画操作系统缺陷特征，并据此提出改进缺陷检测与调试的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统是上层软件的基础，其缺陷可能引发系统异常与数据损坏，影响范围广。系统理解缺陷的分布、触发方式、严重程度及修复模式，是提升操作系统质量保障能力的前提。

Method: 研究收集三个具有代表性的移动端与桌面端操作系统缺陷样本，按照缺陷所在系统层次、受影响功能、触发方式、严重程度和修复涉及的代码元素等维度进行分类统计；进一步开展维度间联合分析，以及跨操作系统的相似性比较。

Result: 研究形成了对不同操作系统缺陷共性与差异的综合刻画，识别出缺陷在层次、功能、触发条件、影响程度和修复代码模式上的重要分布规律。摘要未给出具体比例、显著性检验结果或各项具体发现，因此无法进一步量化结论。

Conclusion: 该工作以较大规模样本补充了操作系统缺陷实证研究，为面向高风险层次、典型触发场景和常见修复模式设计检测、定位与调试技术提供依据，并可作为后续操作系统质量保障研究的经验性指南。

Abstract: Operating systems (OS) serve as the foundation for all other software systems, and thus defects in OSes can lead to severe conquences, such as system crashes and data corruption, affecting billions of users. This broad impact underscores the necessity and importance of ensuring OS quality. Understanding the characteristics of OS defects is a fundamental step in this quality assurance task, as it facilitates the design of effective defect detection and debugging approaches. In this work, we conduct a large-scale study of 1,500 defects from three distinct and representative operating systems (Android, Linux, and HarmonyOS) spanning both mobile and desktop environments. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest study of its kind in this domain. By analyzing the distribution of OS defects across multiple classification dimensions, including the OS layer where defects occur, the functions they affect, how they are triggered, their severity, and the code elements involved in their repair, as well as performing joint analysis between dimensions and cross-OS similarity comparisons, we summarize several major findings that contribute to a comprehensive understanding of OS defects across systems. Based on these findings, we provide a series of actionable implications for better OS defect detection and debugging, offering guidelines for future research aimed at improving OS quality assurance.

</details>


### [8] [AI with Authority, from Application to Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21356)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文主张生成式AI将形式化验证从高成本环节转变为自主开发的必要基础设施，并报告由单名研究者协调多个AI代理完成从应用代码到流片处理器的端到端验证案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器验证成本高、覆盖范围有限，难以适配快速迭代的软件与硬件开发。作者试图证明：若将不可绕过的证明内核置于代理协作链路中，人类可主要负责规格、架构与裁决，而将大规模实现和证明工作交由AI执行。

Method: 提出“Salt方法”：代理间传递的数学主张必须以可由Lean 4内核检查的证明对象形式存在；验证按链路展开，从定理证明内核、经验证编译器和执行环境，到芯片边界的SAT等价性检查。论文还声称公开定理来源、预注册令牌计量、人类工时下界及错误台账，以审计AI参与过程。

Result: 作者声称在五周内，以消费级AI订阅和一名研究者的协调，完成未由人类编写RTL、且证明未经人工逐条审阅的RISC-V处理器设计与社区流片流程；错误台账记录至#256，而进入正式记录的错误证明数为零。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将形式化证明内核定位为AI代理系统的可信协调机制，而非单纯的后验验证工具。该结论的可信度取决于完整工件、证明可复现性、芯片测试结果、计量口径以及错误台账独立审计；仅凭摘要尚无法确认端到端主张的实际范围与外部有效性。

Abstract: For sixty years, machine verification has been a major cost overhead, affordable only for exceptional artifacts. Here we report that generative AI inverts this relationship: at AI speed, machine verification is not only economical but essential to productivity --- it is the incorruptible referee that lets one person safely direct autonomous machine work at scale. In five weeks, one researcher on consumer AI subscriptions directed a small fleet of AI agents from application code, through a verified compiler and executive, to a RISC-V processor taped out on a community silicon shuttle; no proof passed through human review, and no RTL was written by a human. The working discipline --- the Salt method --- rests on a proof kernel no hallucinated proof can pass: mathematical claims travel between agents as kernel-checked artifacts, and human attention is reserved for statements, designs, and rulings. Verification is stated link by link, from the Lean 4 kernel to SAT-checked equivalence at the silicon boundary. We publish the complete accounting: theorem provenance, a pre-registered token meter, floor-bounded human time, and an error ledger whose catch numbering runs to #256 --- a monotone counter over the mathematics campaign's append-only flags ledger, maintained 2026-07-07 to 2026-07-20 (one number, #79, was never assigned; later catches are recorded un-numbered) --- against zero incorrect proofs reaching the record.

</details>


### [9] [Temporal Validity on Real Software Histories: Eliminating Stale-Fact Errors in Code-Assistant Memory over GitHub Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20685)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文在真实软件演化场景中验证了确定性“覆盖关系”记忆机制 MemStrata。针对代码会话中同一事实的新旧值并存、传统 RAG 无法判断当前版本的问题，MemStrata 在130个干净原子状态迁移样本上显著优于 RAG，并几乎消除了输出过期值的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RAG 主要依据语义相似度检索，缺乏显式时间或状态覆盖建模。当函数名、接口位置或依赖版本发生变更时，新旧描述往往高度相似，导致系统可能检索并回答已失效的信息。此前工作仅在合成的单值任务中证明了覆盖记忆的有效性，因此本文旨在检验该机制在真实软件历史中的端到端表现。

Method: 作者从707个真实软件问题中抽取130个“干净原子状态迁移”：一次修复仅将一个可识别值由修复前形式变为修复后形式。数据被处理为无显式时间标记的形式，使新旧陈述仅在取值上不同。实验比较 MemStrata、常规 RAG 及带大模型重排序器的 RAG，评估回答准确率、被迫作答时输出过期值的比例和检索延迟。

Result: MemStrata 的回答准确率为0.91，而 RAG 为0.57至0.59。RAG 在被迫回答时有36%至38%的概率给出已被覆盖的旧值；加入大模型重排序器也未改善这一结构性错误。MemStrata 将过期值回答率降至约0，同时保持与 RAG 相近的检索延迟，约2.1秒；大模型重排序方案约需18秒。研究期间还发现并修复了一个真实产品缺陷，即值比较对大小写和标点过于宽松，但修复后核心性能结论仍得到验证。

Conclusion: 对于可表示为单一事实值更新的软件变更，显式维护“主体—关系—对象”覆盖关系的确定性记忆，能够有效解决 RAG 对新旧事实混淆的问题，且具有较低延迟。结论的适用范围明确受限：干净原子迁移仅约占真实修复的18%，其余复杂修复的自动抽取与覆盖建模仍是独立且尚待解决的问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has no model of time: when a fact changes across a coding session - a function is renamed, an endpoint moves, a dependency is bumped - RAG retrieves both the old and new value with near-identical similarity and cannot tell which is current, so it serves the superseded value. Paper 1 showed, on synthetic single-value benchmarks, that a deterministic (subject, relation, object) supersession memory eliminates this failure. Here we validate it end-to-end on real software history. From 707 real GitHub issues (SWE-bench Lite + Verified) we extract 130 clean atomic state transitions, a fix that changes one identifiable value from a pre-fix to a post-fix form, and render each marker-free (the stale and current statements differ only in the value). On this set, MemStrata reaches 0.91 answer accuracy versus RAG's 0.57-0.59; and, the structural result, when forced to answer RAG serves the superseded value 36-38% of the time (an LLM reranker does not help) while MemStrata drives this to ~0, at RAG retrieval latency (~2.1 s vs ~18 s for the reranker). We are explicit about scope: only ~18% of real fixes are clean atomic transitions; Paper 2 isolates the memory mechanism on that class, and extraction coverage of the remaining fixes is the orthogonal problem we defer to follow-on work. A real product bug surfaced and was fixed during the study (a case/punctuation-insensitive value comparison), with the moat property (deterministic-supersession accuracy on clean code mutations) preserved and verified.

</details>


### [10] [BC-Bench: Evaluating Agentic Engineering in a Domain-Specific Language for ERP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20851)
*Haoran Sun,Klaus Marius Hansen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出BC-Bench，用于评估智能体工程系统在Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central的AL领域专用语言上的真实ERP开发能力。基准包含101个来自两个生产代码库的人工整理任务，并覆盖代码修复、测试生成及含视觉上下文的多模态问题。实验表明，通用软件工程基准上的进步并不能稳定迁移到AL场景，领域专用评测具有必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体工程系统虽在通用基准上表现良好，但其在ERP领域专用语言中的能力缺乏系统研究。AL生态存在公开资料有限、开发环境部署复杂、业务任务常依赖视觉信息等特点，使通用评测难以充分反映实际ERP研发工作流。

Method: 作者借鉴SWE-Bench的思路构建BC-Bench，从两个生产仓库提取并人工筛选101项真实任务。基准针对AL生态适配了资源受限和环境配置复杂等约束；除功能代码生成外，还评估测试生成能力，并支持多模态任务描述。研究在两种智能体执行框架下测试多个前沿模型，采用多次运行指标以减轻生成过程随机性带来的影响。

Result: 在缺陷修复类别中，所测模型之间的解决率差异大于两种智能体框架之间的差异，说明模型本身对表现的影响更显著。同时，模型在通用软件工程基准上报告的能力提升并未持续、稳定地转化为AL任务性能。

Conclusion: BC-Bench为AL和ERP开发场景提供了更贴近生产实践的智能体评测工具。论文的核心结论是：通用基准不能替代领域专用基准，评估ERP代码智能体时应同时考虑真实仓库任务、环境可复现性、测试生成、多模态上下文及随机性稳健评测。

Abstract: Agentic engineering systems have shown strong performance on general-purpose benchmarks, yet their effectiveness in enterprise resource planning (ERP) domain-specific languages (DSLs) remains underexplored. We introduce BC-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate agentic engineering on real-world tasks in AL, the DSL for Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central. BC-Bench comprises 101 manually curated tasks extracted from two Microsoft-owned production repositories, reflecting authentic ERP development workflows. Adapting the SWE-Bench methodology, we address the unique constraints of the AL ecosystem---including limited public resources and complex environment provisioning. Beyond generating functional code, BC-Bench evaluates test generation and supports multimodal problem statements where visual context is commonly present. We evaluate multiple frontier models across two agent harnesses, utilizing multi-run metrics to account for nondeterminism. In the Bug Fixing category, under our evaluated settings, between-model differences in resolution rate are larger than differences between the two evaluated agent harnesses, and improvements reported on general-purpose benchmarks do not consistently transfer to AL. These results highlight the need for domain-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond the Traceback: Using LLMs for Adaptive Explanations of Programming Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20896)
*Alexandru-Radu Moraru,Shreyan Biswas,Ujwal Gadiraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM重写的Python错误信息能显著提升可读性并降低认知负担，但未显著改善实际调试表现；因此，错误解释应根据用户实时修复过程动态调整，而非仅按技能水平静态定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新手程序员往往难以理解标准错误信息。尽管LLM可以生成更易读的解释，但可读性提升是否能转化为更高的调试效率，以及不同解释风格是否应匹配不同技能水平，仍缺乏实证证据。

Method: 开展多阶段众包研究，共103名参与者。研究首先通过定制能力测评划分参与者技能水平，随后比较标准解释器错误信息与两种LLM生成风格：强调行动建议的务实型解释，以及提供分层引导的情境型解释。评估指标包括修复率、尝试次数、修复时间，以及可读性、认知负荷和语气等主观感受。

Result: LLM重写信息在主观评价上显著优于标准错误信息，其中务实型解释被认为更清晰且认知负荷更低。但这些主观优势并未带来修复率、尝试次数或修复时间等客观调试指标的统计显著改善。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人机互补性缺口：更易读、更令人满意的解释不一定能提升实际调试能力。未来自适应反馈系统应减少对静态技能定向重写的依赖，转而依据用户实时的修复轨迹动态调整解释内容与引导方式。

Abstract: Programming error messages are critical for software development, yet they remain difficult for novice programmers to interpret. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can rewrite these errors into clearer explanations, it remains unclear whether increased readability improves objective debugging performance or how explanation styles should align with programmer skill. We present a multi-stage crowdsourced study N=103 evaluating skill-targeted, LLM-generated Python error messages. Using a custom proficiency assessment, we categorized participants by skill level and tested standard interpreter messages against two LLM-generated styles: pragmatic (action-oriented) and contingent (scaffolded explanations). We measured both objective debugging metrics (fix rate, attempts, time-to-fix) and subjective perceptions (readability, cognitive load, tone). Our results show that while LLM-rewritten messages significantly improved subjective evaluations, with pragmatic messages rated as clearer and less cognitively demanding, these perceived gains did not translate into statistically significant improvements in objective debugging performance. This highlights a critical human-AI complementarity gap: explanations that feel better to users do not necessarily make them more effective debuggers. We discuss design implications for adaptive AI feedback systems, arguing that future tools should pivot from static skill-targeted rewriting toward dynamic adjustments based on a user's real-time repair trajectory.

</details>


### [12] [Generation of Web Apps with Agentic IDEs: An Empirical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20903)
*Manuel Marceca,Maria Teresa Rossi,Leonardo Mariani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文比较了Copilot、Cursor和Windsurf三种智能体式IDE从零生成五个全栈Web应用的能力，发现其对CRUD与认证等成熟开发模式表现较好，但在任务队列等较少见的分布式架构上错误显著增多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对智能体式IDE的评估多未覆盖端到端、完整应用生成场景。论文旨在通过统一的全栈开发任务，补充不同工具在实际应用构建能力上的系统性比较证据。

Method: 研究以从零构建五个全栈Web应用为基准任务，对三种主流智能体式IDE进行对比，考察其生成不同功能与架构模式时的完成质量、错误情况及工具间差异。

Result: 三种工具在常见且模式化程度高的功能上具有较高成熟度，能够较好生成CRUD操作和身份认证功能；面对任务队列等非典型分布式架构时，生成错误明显增加。不同IDE存在各自特性，但总体性能差距较小。

Conclusion: 智能体式IDE尚不能替代开发者，但会改变开发者职责：开发者更侧重于以自然语言指令编排智能体，并通过迭代式审查和修正完成软件构建。其适用性目前更强于成熟、常规的软件模式，而复杂或较少见的架构仍需要开发者进行严格验证与干预。

Abstract: Agentic IDEs are among the most significant innovations in software engineering, aiming to accelerate application development through LLM-based agents that can assist developers during development. However, their evaluation in end-to-end development tasks involving the generation of complete applications remains limited. To fill this gap, we propose a rigorous comparative analysis of three popular agentic IDEs (Copilot, Cursor, and Windsurf) in the generation of five full-stack Web applications from scratch.
  Results show high maturity in the generation of established patterns, such as CRUD operations and authentication features. In contrast, the generation of less common distributed architectures, such as a task queue architecture, produces significantly more errors. Overall, results show that Agentic IDEs cannot replace developers but shift their role toward building software by orchestrating LLM-based agents through natural-language instructions and iterative refinement. Yet, each agentic IDE shows its peculiarities, although differences are narrow.

</details>


### [13] [Spike-Killer: Evidence-Gated LLM Assistance for Safe Performance Diagnosis on a Real Windows Workstation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21069)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: LLM-assisted agents can synthesize system evidence, propose configuration changes, and automate diagnostic tasks, but their flexibility makes an imprecise action or an intrusive collector an operational risk. We present Spike-Killer, a human-approved workflow for diagnosing frame-time complaints on one real Windows workstation. The workflow treats each action as an evidence-gated transaction: it records the exact target state, classifies risk, preserves a snapshot, verifies a postcondition, and retains failed measurements as first-class evidence.
  This experience paper reports a completed same-day study with Counter-Strike 2 as a demanding target application. The evidence bundle contains preserved state snapshots, exploratory microbenchmarks, a ten-run same-state repeatability probe, live telemetry, a repaired over-broad registry action, incompatible presentation-capture attempts, an invalid local replay, and a system-level tracing replacement. Windows Performance Recorder produced two CS2 local-Bot GPU traces of 90.69 and 85.85 seconds; both were attributed to cs2.exe, exposed DxgKrnl Present metadata, and had zero lost ETW buffers or events. These results qualify trace integrity, not performance: the study reports no frame intervals, P99 estimate, or intervention effect. The contribution is an auditable, human-in-the-loop pattern for trustworthy agent assistance on a real workstation, including explicit stop conditions when evidence is insufficient.

</details>


### [14] [Trustworthy RAG: An Evaluation Agent for Detecting Misinformation and Knowledge Poisoning in Generative AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21095)
*Balkrishna Giri,Md Toufique Hasan,Jussi Rasku,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向RAG的生成前上下文可信评估代理，通过事实核验、投毒检测与信任指数降低检索文档被恶意污染后诱导模型输出错误信息的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG往往将语义相关性等同于可信性，忽略检索内容可能包含错误、篡改或指令注入，导致“安全—可靠性缺口”。研究旨在对上下文进行独立评估，而非盲目信任检索结果。

Method: 方法由三部分构成：基于自然语言推理的事实一致性验证；融合五类信号并按相关性加权的投毒检测器；信任指数T=0.4F+0.35C+0.25(1-P)，其中F、C、P分别对应事实性、一致性与投毒风险，并在高污染场景施加非线性抑制。系统还针对不同LLM进行阈值校准。

Result: 在TruthfulQA与Llama 3.3 70B上，代理达到91%准确率和100%精确率；对指令注入的召回率为100%。跨三种LLM时，信任指数的ROC-AUC为0.73至0.81，且生成风格对效果的影响大于模型规模。每模型阈值校准可恢复有竞争力的准确率，但在FEVER上的较弱结果表明跨数据集迁移需要领域校准。软件工程实验中，对OWASP Top 10与CWE资料的安全编码助手可有效拦截不安全建议的指令注入，F1为92%。实体替换、上下文矛盾和细微语义弱化仍较难识别。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，生成前的上下文可信度评估能够显著增强RAG对明显投毒和指令注入的防护能力，但其评估对象是污染上下文的检测，而非模型是否实际采纳错误信息。方法的主要局限在于对隐蔽编辑和跨领域数据的鲁棒性不足，后续应加强细粒度语义篡改识别、领域自适应校准及端到端生成影响评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds Large Language Model (LLM) outputs in external knowledge, but RAG systems usually trust whatever they retrieve, creating a Security-Reliability Gap: high semantic relevance does not guarantee factual truth. Adversaries exploit this through knowledge poisoning, inserting malicious documents to cause targeted misinformation. We propose an Evaluation Agent, middleware that combines Natural Language Inference (NLI) factual verification, a five-signal poison detector with relevance-weighted aggregation, and a Trust Index T = 0.4 F + 0.35 C + 0.25 (1 - P ) with a non-linear dampener for high-contamination contexts. On TruthfulQA with Llama 3.3 70B, the agent reaches 91% accuracy and 100% precision, with 100% recall on instruction injection, while in-place edits, such as entity swaps, remain hard to detect. Across three LLMs the Trust Index stays discriminative, with a Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.73 to 0.81; generation style matters more than model size, and per-LLM threshold calibration restores baseline competitive accuracy, whereas a weaker FEVER result shows that cross-dataset generalization requires domain-specific calibration. In a software-engineering use case, a secure-coding assistant over guidance from the Open Worldwide Application Security Project (OWASP) Top 10 and the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), the agent reliably blocks instruction injection of unsafe advice (F1 92%), while contradiction and subtle semantic weakening remain hard. Throughout, the agent measures detection of poisoned context before generation, not whether the LLM adopts the injected misinformation. We release the proposed approach, attack generator, and experimental artifacts at the link: https://github.com/GPT-Laboratory/TrustworthyRAG.

</details>


### [15] [On the Time and Frequency Domain Representations of Signals for CPS Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21167)
*Claudio Mandrioli,Drishti Yadav,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出信号频谱时序逻辑（S2TL），利用时频表示描述和监测网络物理系统（CPS）中的频率区间及频率成分关系。实验表明，相比时域规范，S2TL适用范围更广，并对偏置与高频噪声具有更强容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS规范语言主要采用时域信号表示，难以准确描述信号形状和动态行为；控制与机器人领域常用的频域表示虽能刻画这些特征，但缺乏时间信息。因此，需要融合时间和频率信息的规范表示方法。

Method: 分析CPS需求分类体系，识别适合采用时频表示的需求类别；提出S2TL语言，用于表达频率区间上的断言及频率成分之间的关系；形式化其语义并实现CPS轨迹监测器；针对目标需求类别设计规范模板，并从适用性、表达保真度和噪声容忍度方面比较时域与时频域规范。

Result: 时域规范通常仅适用于包含阶跃变化或采用常值插值的输入轨迹，而时频规范还能处理由线性插值生成的轨迹。时频规范能够提高对偏置和高频噪声的容忍度，同时保持与目标系统属性相当的表达保真度。

Conclusion: 时频表示适合用于描述一类具有动态频谱特征的CPS需求。S2TL及其监测器为此类需求提供了可操作的规范与验证手段，在轨迹插值形式和噪声鲁棒性方面优于传统时域规范。

Abstract: Specification languages are instrumental to the Verification \& Validation of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). Most state-of-the-art specification languages use the time-domain representation of signals, which is not always suitable for describing signal shapes and dynamic behaviours. Instead, fields like control and robotics use the frequency-domain representation to characterise these behaviours. Time-frequency representations combine the capabilities of both domains. We investigate the use of time-frequency representations to specify CPS requirements. We analyse existing taxonomies of CPS requirements to identify which requirement classes can benefit from time-frequency representations. We derive the desiderata for a specification language that uses time-frequency representations and propose Signal-Spectrum Temporal Logic (S2TL), a language enabling assertions over frequency intervals and relations between frequency components. We operationalise the S2TL semantics for monitoring CPS traces, and implement a monitor. We define specification templates for the identified requirement classes and compare time- and time-frequency-domain formulations in terms of applicability, expression fidelity, and noise tolerance of monitoring. We observe that, while time-domain specifications are applicable only to input traces containing step-like changes or using constant interpolation, time-frequency specifications extend their evaluation to traces generated through linear interpolation, and improve tolerance to offset and high-frequency noise, while achieving comparable fidelity to the intended system properties.

</details>


### [16] [The Substitution Escrow Threshold: When "Compatible With" Becomes Safe Enough to Buy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21221)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“替代托管阈值”（Substitution Escrow Threshold）框架，用于区分兼容性声明究竟能降低长期替代风险，还是仅降低首次集成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业常将“S3 兼容”“PostgreSQL 兼容”或“OpenAI 兼容”等声明视为未来可迁移、可替代的保证，但此类声明通常未覆盖数据、运行状态、运维能力及专有扩展等迁移前提，因此容易形成隐性锁定。

Method: 作者构建五项判定条件：边界闭合、可执行一致性、控制权独立、状态与运维可逆性、扩展隔离；并将其应用于 OCI、Kubernetes、OpenTelemetry、S3 和 PostgreSQL 五类基础设施案例，以比较不同兼容性主张对应的替代保障程度。

Result: 框架将五个案例映射到五种不同结果类型，表明“兼容”并非单一属性：接口或协议一致不必然意味着系统可替换。只有同时满足关键条件，兼容性才能构成可信的制度性风险缓释；否则其价值主要限于降低接入与开发摩擦。

Conclusion: 该研究为企业架构师、平台工程团队、采购人员和投资者提供了评估兼容性依赖的操作性诊断工具。其核心贡献是将兼容性评价从接口层提升到迁移路径、控制权和运维连续性层面；面向 AI 基础设施的兼容性承诺是最紧迫的后续应用方向。

Abstract: Enterprise infrastructure buyers routinely evaluate compatibility claims--"S3-compatible," "PostgreSQL-compatible," "OpenAI compatible"--as proxies for future substitution options. Yet most compatibility claims do not escrow the substitution path they imply. This paper introduces the Substitution Escrow Threshold, a five-condition framework that determines when a compatibility claim genuinely reduces institutional risk versus merely reducing first-integration cost. The five conditions--boundary closure, executable conformance, custody independence, state and operations reversibility, and extension quarantine--are applied to five infrastructure cases (OCI, Kubernetes, OpenTelemetry, S3, PostgreSQL) that populate five distinct outcome cells. The framework produces actionable diagnostics for enterprise architects, platform engineers, procurement teams, and investors evaluating compatibility-dependent infrastructure decisions, and identifies AI infrastructure as the framework's most urgent next application.

</details>


### [17] [Human-AI Collaboration in Requirements Engineering: Evidence of the Negative Effect of LLMs on Requirements Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21298)
*Giovanna Broccia,Julian Frattini,Chetan Arora,Maurice H. ter Beek,Alessandro Fantechi,Andreas Vogelsang,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过34名参与者的交叉实验评估LLM辅助需求检查的效果。结果显示，LLM支持降低了需求异味识别准确率，对严重性分类和检查时长未产生显著影响，并可能削弱初学者在需求检查中的学习增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求检查可在软件生命周期早期发现需求工件缺陷，而LLM正被用于辅助需求工程任务。但LLM作为人类协作助手是否能切实提升人工需求检查的有效性与效率，尤其是对初学检查者的影响，仍缺乏可靠的实证证据。

Method: 研究采用受控交叉设计：34名参与者分别在有LLM支持和无LLM支持条件下检查文本规格说明，识别需求异味、将其分类为有害或无害，并记录任务耗时。针对异味识别、严重性分类和检查时长三个结果变量，研究分别构建贝叶斯回归模型，同时控制交叉实验可能引入的有效性威胁、协变量及中介因素。

Result: LLM支持对需求异味检测准确率产生负向影响；对异味严重性分类准确性及任务完成时长没有显著影响。实验期间存在学习效应，但当参与者先在LLM支持条件下执行需求检查时，该学习效应较弱。

Conclusion: LLM辅助并不必然提升人工需求检查表现；对于经验不足的检查者，它还可能妨碍异味识别。研究进一步提示，若从学习初期即依赖LLM，需求检查技能的习得速度可能下降。因此，在将LLM引入需求检查培训或实践时，应审慎设计其使用方式，并避免将其视为无条件有效的性能增强工具。

Abstract: Background. Requirements inspection (RI) is a well-established practice for detecting potential defects in requirements artifacts early in the software lifecycle. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have stimulated interest in their potential to support requirements engineering (RE) tasks. However, empirical evidence on the effects of LLMs when used as collaborative assistants in human-performed RI remains scarce. Aims. We aim to investigate the impact of LLM support on human-performed RI, considering inspection effectiveness in terms of smell identification and severity classification (i.e., nocuous vs innocuous), as well as inspection duration. Method. We conducted a controlled crossover design experiment with 34 participants, who inspected textual specifications with and without LLM support, identifying and classifying requirements smells while recording inspection time. We analyzed the data using one Bayesian regression model per outcome variable, accounting for validity threats induced by the crossover design as well as covariates and mediators. Results. Results show that LLM support negatively affects smell detection accuracy but has no significant effect on smell classification or task duration. A learning effect is present across experimental periods, but reduced when RI is first performed with LLM support. Conclusions. Our findings provide empirical evidence that LLM support does not necessarily improve performance and may, instead, hinder it for novice inspectors. Moreover, the results suggest that learning RI with LLM-support from the beginning may slow down the skill acquisition process, implying threats for LLM-supported learning.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Fault Localization: A Trajectory-Level Study of LLM Agents for Microservice Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21310)
*Qisheng Lu,Aoyang Fang,Junjielong Xu,Jin'ao Shang,Songhan Zhang,Yifan Yang,Xiaochuan Yan,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向微服务自动根因分析的轨迹级评估框架：除检查最终定位的服务是否正确外，还评估智能体是否基于可观察证据重建了从故障源到症状的传播路径。基于此框架提出DiagGuard，在独立实验中将Acc@1从43.5%提升至52.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动根因分析评测主要采用端点正确性，即是否定位到责任服务。该指标无法揭示诊断结论的证据基础，也无法反映故障传播链路；因此即使最终答案正确，运维人员仍难判断结论是否可信、是否值得执行处置。

Method: 作者将根因分析定义为可观测的诊断过程，并以人工整理的服务级故障传播路径为参照，对3,500条诊断轨迹进行评估。分析维度包括智能体的调查位置、对检索到的遥测数据的使用方式及查询策略演化。进一步将发现归纳为诊断质量分类，并实现DiagGuard双阶段架构：先通过grounding调查可用观测信息，再由verification依据这些信息审计定位结论。

Result: 轨迹分析发现，最终定位正确性与诊断质量存在脱节：智能体可能找对故障源，但未能重建故障传播过程。高质量诊断通常聚焦于故障影响面、直接利用检索证据，并随搜索深入扩展查询类型。失败模式主要包括遗漏决定性证据、误读已检索证据，以及在证据不足时进行无依据推断。在不同模型、基准和服务拓扑的独立环境中，DiagGuard使Acc@1由43.5%提高至52.5%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，仅以最终答案衡量自动根因分析会掩盖证据不足和推理链断裂等关键缺陷。轨迹级评估能够更可靠地衡量诊断可信度，并为改进智能体的证据检索、解释和验证机制提供可操作依据；DiagGuard验证了将证据覆盖与结论审计纳入流程可提升根因定位性能。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of automated root cause analysis (RCA) for microservices assess diagnostic performance mainly by endpoint correctness: whether a method localizes the responsible service. This criterion enables comparison but does not reveal the evidentiary basis of a diagnosis or the fault-propagation route connecting the source to observed symptoms, both of which an on-call site reliability engineer needs to judge whether action is warranted. We therefore treat RCA as an observable diagnostic process. Our trajectory-level framework evaluates agent executions against manually curated service-level fault-propagation paths. Applied to a public microservice RCA benchmark, it analyzes 3,500 diagnostic trajectories, characterizing where agents investigate and how they use retrieved telemetry. We find a disconnect between answer correctness and diagnostic quality: an agent may localize the fault source yet fail to reconstruct its propagation. Successful investigations stay on the fault-impact surface, act on retrieved evidence, and broaden their query repertoire as the search deepens. Failures arise when decisive evidence is omitted, retrieved evidence is misinterpreted, or unsupported inference substitutes for missing evidence. We operationalize this taxonomy as DiagGuard, a two-stage defense-in-depth architecture in which grounding surveys available observations before localization and verification audits the diagnosis against them. In an independent setting with a different model, benchmark, and service topology, DiagGuard raises Acc@1 from 43.5% to 52.5%. These results show that trajectory-level evaluation exposes limitations hidden by final-answer metrics and provides actionable guidance for improving automated RCA.

</details>


### [19] [AI-to-AI Code Reviews of GitHub Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21311)
*Niruthiha Selvanayagam,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文构建了大规模AI作者—AI审查者代码评审数据集，发现闭环AI-to-AI评审增长迅速，但在已识别的智能体活动中仍占少数；不同产品组合的评审输出和响应时延存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码智能体同时参与拉取请求（PR）的创建与评审，软件工程中出现“一个AI审查另一个AI贡献”的闭环机制。论文旨在量化该现象的规模、增长趋势，以及不同作者—审查者组合下的评审行为差异。

Method: 作者基于公开CodAGE数据集，将带有AI归因的PR与带有AI归因的评审事件关联，得到248,641个至少接受过一次AI评审的唯一PR。研究按跨产品与同产品组合分组，统计规模、时间趋势、评论类别、每PR评论数及时间戳可用样本中的评审延迟，并对组间差异及效应量进行比较。

Result: 数据集中45,269个PR获得跨产品评审，208,145个获得同产品评审，4,773个同时属于两类。跨产品评审约占已识别智能体作者PR的1.6%，但从2025年第一季度至第三季度增长超过两个数量级。CodeRabbit对Claude Code作者PR的评论中，35.0%标为重构类，而对Copilot作者PR仅为10.5%，但该差异可能由PR自身特征驱动。四个兼具作者和审查者角色的产品中，有三个在同产品组的平均每PR评论数高58%至65%，但总体效应量较小或可忽略，差异主要集中于分布上尾。时间戳完整且非负的样本中，跨产品组中位延迟为1.2分钟，同产品组为4.7分钟；该比较受时间戳缺失模式及审查者构成差异限制。

Conclusion: AI-to-AI代码评审已形成可观测且快速扩张的闭环，但尚非主导模式。评审内容、评论密度与时延会随作者—审查者产品组合变化，不过现有结果主要呈现相关性，不能直接归因于产品行为；未来研究应控制PR复杂度、仓库特征、时间戳缺失和审查者选择机制。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, operating on both sides of the pull-request (PR) process: AI authoring agents create or modify PRs, while AI reviewers evaluate them. This creates a closed loop in which one AI coding agent reviews a contribution attributed to another. We construct a large-scale dataset of AI-to-AI code review by linking AI-attributed PRs with AI-attributed review events from CodAGE, a public dataset of coding-agent-generated GitHub events. Our dataset contains 248,641 unique AI-attributed PRs that received at least one AI-attributed review. Of these, 45,269 received cross-product review and 208,145 received same-product review; 4,773 PRs received both. Cross-product AI-to-AI review occurred in approximately 1.6% of identified agent-authored PRs but was substantial in absolute terms, and its volume increased by more than two orders of magnitude from 2025-Q1 to 2025-Q3. Reviewer output varied across author-reviewer configurations. CodeRabbit labeled 35.0% of its comments on Claude Code-authored PRs as refactor comments, compared with 10.5% on Copilot-authored PRs, although this difference may reflect characteristics of the PRs rather than the reviewer. For three of four dual-role reviewers, mean comments per PR were 58-65% higher in the same-product group, although effect sizes were small or negligible and the difference was concentrated in the upper tail. Among pairs with complete, nonnegative timestamps, the observed median latency was 1.2 minutes for cross-product pairs and 4.7 minutes for same-product pairs; differential timestamp availability and reviewer composition limit this comparison. Overall, closed-loop AI-to-AI review is increasing but remains a minority of identified agent activity, with review output varying across authoring-agent groups and product configurations.

</details>


### [20] [Natural-Language Workflows Are Not Software Yet: Artifact-Driven Compilation for Reliable Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21341)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hanxi Guo,Guangyu Shen,Siyuan Cheng,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Artic，一种由大语言模型辅助的工件驱动工作流编译器。它将自然语言工作流转为显式声明数据读写、产物约束和控制转移的结构化工作流，从而提升智能体执行的可靠性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言工作流虽便于领域专家编写和复用，但通常隐含步骤间的数据依赖，并包含长程或分支控制逻辑。执行智能体需自行推断应使用哪些历史结果，在上下文压力下容易遗漏依赖或偏离流程，导致任务完成率和执行一致性不足。

Method: Artic将原始工作流编译为工件驱动表示：每一步显式定义读取与写入的工件，利用约束对生成工件进行门控，并以明确的控制转移组织执行路径。系统据此暴露每个步骤对智能体的执行负担，识别状态依赖过多或控制逻辑复杂的步骤，并通过受约束优化进行细化。为验证编译忠实性，Artic将验证拆解为局部义务，并采用基于场景的干运行检查已编译区域是否符合源工作流。

Result: 在来自11个真实领域工作流的488个问题实例上，Artic相较原始文本工作流将任务解决率提高28个百分点。其编译后的工作流在跨模型设置下的一致性提高32个百分点，在重复执行设置下的一致性提高56个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式工件依赖、产物约束和控制流能够有效降低智能体执行自然语言流程时的推理与记忆负担。Artic证明了将自然语言工作流编译为可验证的工件驱动表示，可显著改善任务成功率、模型间可迁移性及重复运行的稳定性。

Abstract: Natural-language workflows offer a software-like interface for agents: domain experts can write reusable procedures, and agents can execute them as instructions. This promise is not yet reliable. Workflow descriptions often leave data dependencies implicit, so the executor must infer which prior results a step should use; agents can also fail to follow long or branching instructions under context pressure. We propose Artic, an artifact-driven workflow compiler that transforms a natural-language workflow into an artifact-driven workflow in which each step declares the artifacts it reads and writes, constraints gate produced artifacts, and explicit control transfers route execution. This representation exposes the enforcement burden placed on agent execution, allowing the compiler to identify steps that depend on too much state or contain difficult control logic and refine them through constrained optimization. To validate the LLM-assisted transformation, Artic decomposes faithfulness checking into local obligations and uses scenario-based dry runs to test whether compiled workflow regions conform to the source workflow. We evaluate Artic on 488 problem instances from 11 real-world domain workflows; it improves task resolve rate by 28 percentage points over the original text workflow. We also show that workflows compiled by Artic are 32 and 56 percentage points more consistent in cross-model and repeated-execution setups, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [21] [AEGIS: Preventing Cross-Domain Resource Abuse in MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20481)
*Shriti Priya,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AEGIS：面向模型上下文协议（MCP）的细粒度资源滥用策略执行组件。其核心是利用大语言模型将异构、多模态工具调用归一化为统一的策略表示，并结合Open Policy Agent与ContextForge AI Gateway，对可能造成后端资源过载的请求进行检测和约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP工具使智能体能够调用文本、图像、视频、位置等外部能力，但不同模态具有不同的资源消耗方式。例如过大的检索范围或过长的视频请求可能显著增加计算、存储、带宽或服务响应负担。由于跨工具请求模式和参数差异大，传统静态规则难以同时实现跨场景通用性与参数级精确控制，因此需要统一且可扩展的策略治理机制。

Method: AEGIS首先借助大语言模型的语义推理能力，对不同MCP工具调用进行分析、分类和参数语义归一化，形成面向策略定义的统一中间表示。管理员可据此定义细粒度资源约束策略。系统再与Open Policy Agent集成以执行策略决策，并通过ContextForge AI Gateway在工具调用链路中实施拦截、限制或缓解措施。该设计旨在覆盖异构工具、不同模态及跨领域调用，同时保留MCP生态的工具扩展灵活性。

Result: 摘要声称，AEGIS能够识别并缓解MCP智能体应用中的资源滥用行为，并为安全管理人员提供可访问的统一策略接口。其预期效果是在不依赖每类工具专门规则的前提下，对多模态工具请求实施一致的资源治理。摘要未给出实验数据、基准任务、误报率、性能开销或与现有方案的定量比较，因此实际有效性和部署成本仍需通过正文验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS将大语言模型驱动的工具调用语义归一化与策略引擎执行结合，为MCP环境中的资源滥用防护提供了通用架构。论文的主要贡献在于解决异构多模态工具参数难以统一治理的问题；其关键挑战则包括归一化结果的可靠性、策略可解释性、推理延迟与成本，以及面对新型工具或模糊调用时的稳健性。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open source JSON-RPC protocol that standardizes how large language models (LLMs) interact with external systems through programmatic functions known as tools. Attackers or malicious agents can exploit certain modalities of these MCP tools to degrade the overall quality of service of agent-based applications. For example, an agent may request an excessively large search radius or very long videos, overloading backend systems and potentially causing slowdowns or denial-of-service. Each modality including text, images, video, and location introduces distinct vectors for resource abuse, complicating the development of consistent mitigation strategies. Moreover, multimodal and crossdomain tools expose diverse request schemas and parameters, making it difficult to define policies that are both generalizable and precise enough to enforce meaningful resource constraints. In this paper, we present AEGIS, a policy enforcement component that enables administrators to define fine-grained safeguards against resource abuse across heterogeneous MCP tools and modalities. AEGIS leverages the reasoning capabilities of large language models to analyze, categorize, and normalize diverse tool invocations into a unified, policy-friendly representation accessible to security practitioners. Integrated with the Open Policy Agent and the ContextForge AI Gateway, AEGIS detects and mitigates abusive behaviors while preserving the flexibility of MCP-based agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [22] [aiXamine: Unified Black-Box Evaluation of Cross-Dimensional Trade-offs in LLM Safety, Security, and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20554)
*Fatih Deniz,Yazan Boshmaf,Dorde Popovic,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 aiXamine：一个以黑盒方式联合评估大语言模型安全性、安全防护与隐私性的统一平台。其核心观点是可信性具有跨维耦合与权衡关系，单独优化或评估某一维度无法反映模型的整体风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常将安全、安全防护和隐私割裂处理，因而可能遗漏关键失效模式：例如模型表面安全得分很高，却对大量正常请求过度拒答；或能力提升的同时隐私表现显著下降。作者旨在建立可复现、可横向比较的联合评测框架，以识别这些单维评估不可见的风险与权衡。

Method: aiXamine 通过自动化对抗测试流程，编排覆盖九类服务的46项测试，并为模型生成从提示词级诊断到跨服务权衡分析的分层风险画像。作者在相同测试条件下，对120余个模型进行了超过5,000次测试；同时比较专有模型与开放权重模型，并分析不同可信维度之间的统计关联、规模效应及蒸馏训练造成的鲁棒性变化。

Result: 研究发现三类主要跨维现象：其一，安全约束存在可量化的“安全税”，更强的对齐通常伴随更高的过度拒答率，形成保护性与实用性的权衡；其二，隐私与其他可信维度近乎正交，常规对齐指标不能有效预测隐私表现；其三，作者刻画了蒸馏诱发的鲁棒性崩塌：缺少在线策略校正的离策略蒸馏会导致熵塌缩，使同一基础架构的鲁棒性从56.9降至2.6。论文还观察到扩大模型规模的收益递减，以及不同类别任务上的安全行为差异。

Conclusion: 论文认为，模型可信性不是可由单一目标或单轴指标充分描述的属性；某一维度的改善既不保证其他维度同步提升，也可能损害其他维度。aiXamine 为联合、黑盒、可复现的可信性评估提供了基础设施，并提示后续对齐与训练方法应显式建模安全性、安全防护、隐私和实用性之间的多目标权衡。

Abstract: The critical failure modes in deployed large language models (LLMs) are cross-dimensional: a model can score 99.3 in safety alignment while refusing one in three benign queries, or improve across every capability metric while losing 21 points in privacy. Existing evaluation frameworks that assess safety, security, and privacy independently cannot detect these patterns. We introduce aiXamine, a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across safety, security, and privacy as interdependent properties. aiXamine orchestrates 46 tests across nine services through an automated red-teaming pipeline, producing hierarchical risk profiles, from prompt-level diagnostics to cross-service trade-off analytics, that enable reproducible comparison of proprietary and open-weight systems under identical conditions. Applying aiXamine to over 120 LLMs through more than 5,000 test runs, we conduct the largest joint safety, security, and privacy study to date and uncover three cross-dimensional phenomena invisible to single-axis evaluation. First, safety enforcement incurs a quantifiable safety tax: stronger alignment systematically increases over-refusal, forcing providers to choose between protection and utility. Second, privacy is near-orthogonal to other trustworthiness dimensions and not captured by standard alignment. Third, we identify and formally characterize distillation-induced robustness collapse: off-policy distillation without on-policy correction causes entropy collapse, catastrophically destroying robustness (56.9$\to$2.6) on the same base architecture. These findings, compounded by diminishing returns from scale and category-dependent safety behaviors, demonstrate that trustworthiness is inherently multi-dimensional: progress along one axis does not guarantee, and can actively undermine, progress along others, yet current alignment methods treat it as a single objective.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond End-to-End Success: Diagnosing Failures in Long-Horizon Security LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20563)
*Wei Shao,Chongzhou Fang,Zuxiong Tan,Zequan Liang,Setareh Rafatirad,Avesta Sasan,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向长时程安全类LLM智能体的诊断评估方法：通过任务检查点区分能力暴露前后的失败，并以受控干预验证上游瓶颈。实验显示，不同模型代际的主要失败来源可能显著不同，单看最终成功率不足以解释智能体能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务包含多轮相互依赖的交互，后续决策依赖早期发现的服务、状态或访问条件。最终任务失败可能发生在目标能力真正被调用之前，因此传统的端到端成功率无法判断模型究竟是缺乏目标能力，还是在前置步骤中丢失了必要条件。

Method: 作者在任务中植入检查点，记录智能体是否完成信息发现、状态观察、策略恢复及不确定结果后的决策等关键前提；据此将失败划分为能力暴露前失败与能力暴露后失败。随后针对怀疑的上游障碍实施预先设定的受控干预，并与匹配的非干预对照比较。评估覆盖四类任务：延迟复用已发现信息、复用已观察状态、失败策略后的恢复，以及不确定结果后的决策。

Result: 在“观察状态复用”任务中，检查点分析发现，Gemini 2.5 Flash的大量失败发生在模型尚未观察到后续需要复用的状态之前。在预先设定的92个随机种子实验中，针对协议歧义提供的定向指导将状态观察率从匹配非指导对照下的65.5%提升至95.4%。但相同实验设计用于Gemini 3.7 Flash时得到相反效果，且状态观察不再能稳定预测最终任务完成。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体评估应从聚合成功率转向过程诊断：定位失败发生的阶段、识别其因果瓶颈，并通过干预验证解释。模型更新可能改变失败机制，因此对某一版本有效的提示或修复手段未必可迁移到后续版本；检查点与受控干预可为模型比较和改进提供更可靠的依据。

Abstract: Long-horizon security LLM agents must carry information and decisions across many dependent interactions, where later actions often depend on services, state, or access discovered much earlier. This makes final task success difficult to interpret: an agent may fail before it ever reaches the point where the capability of interest can be exercised. We present a diagnostic methodology that instruments security tasks with checkpoints, separates failures before and after capability exposure, and uses controlled interventions to test suspected upstream bottlenecks. We evaluate the methodology across four task families involving delayed reuse of discovered information, reuse of observed state, recovery from failed strategies, and decision making after uncertain outcomes. On observed state reuse, checkpoint analysis shows that many Gemini 2.5 Flash failures occur before the model observes the state it is later expected to reuse. In a pre-specified 92-seed study, targeted protocol-disambiguation guidance increases state observation from 65.5\% under a matched non-guidance control message to 95.4\%. Repeating the same design with Gemini 3.7 Flash produces the opposite effect, while state observation no longer reliably predicts task completion. These results show that the dominant source of failure can shift across model generations, motivating evaluation that diagnoses where and why long-horizon security agents fail rather than relying only on aggregate task success.

</details>


### [24] [Temporal Risk on Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20575)
*Shiqi Liu,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向卫星系统的时序化安全风险评估框架，将时间作为核心分析维度，以刻画任务不同阶段中风险暴露与攻击影响的动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全风险框架通常将威胁、脆弱性和风险视为静态因素，难以反映轨道变化、功率余量收紧及环境条件变化所导致的风险波动。同一利用方式在关键机动等特定任务窗口可能造成远高于常态运行阶段的影响，因此需要动态评估机制。

Method: 框架扩展既有对手行为分类体系，引入五维时间能力模型；将任务划分为具有运行意义的时间窗口及环境区间，分别估计各窗口内的利用难度、发生可能性和潜在影响。输出不是单一风险分数，而是一组带时间索引的可能性—影响矩阵。

Result: 该方法能够揭示卫星系统在不同任务阶段和环境条件下的相对高风险时段，呈现风险随时间变化的结构化图景，并支持比较同类威胁在不同运行窗口中的利用可行性与后果差异。

Conclusion: 时序化风险评估可为运行方提供更具操作性的安全决策依据：避开高风险窗口安排敏感任务，并依据动态威胁态势调整防护资源部署，从而弥补静态风险模型的不足。

Abstract: Satellite vulnerabilities change over time as orbits shift, power margins tighten, and the space environment deteriorates. However, most cybersecurity risk frameworks still treat threats as static. In practice, the same exploit can be far more damaging during a critical maneuver than during routine operations. We propose a temporal risk assessment framework that makes time an explicit axis in satellite security analysis. It extends existing adversary behavior taxonomies with a five-dimensional temporal capability model and estimates exploitation difficulty across distinct temporal windows of a mission. Rather than producing a single risk score, the framework outputs a series of time-indexed likelihood-impact matrices. It discretizes missions into operationally meaningful time windows and environmental bands to show when systems are most exposed. This view helps operators avoid scheduling sensitive operations in high-risk periods and align defensive resources with a threat landscape that shifts over time.

</details>


### [25] [Keyed Provenance Watermarking with Complementary Lattice-Based Secure Aggregation for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20580)
*Xinyun Liu,Zhi Lu,Yu Chen,Ronghua Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一个端到端可信联邦学习框架，将数据层的上下文来源水印与计算层的基于格的零知识安全聚合结合，以同时缓解数据泄露、未授权复用和恶意梯度操纵问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习防护通常分别处理数据来源、模型更新隐私和异常梯度验证，缺少覆盖数据生成、嵌入、训练及聚合全过程的协同机制。作者旨在构建可追溯、可验证且具备后量子安全性的双层防护体系。

Method: 数据层中，框架从时间、位置和服务器标识等可信基础设施信息构造物理锚定元数据（PAM），再通过客户端密钥驱动的HMAC-SHA-256生成水印载荷；随后利用FMGAN，将特征融合模块与Mamba引导线性注意力结合，实现鲁棒图像水印嵌入。计算层中，采用基于RLWE的格零知识安全聚合协议，在不暴露各客户端梯度的前提下验证密钥正确性、梯度L2范数边界及余弦相似度约束。

Result: 摘要声称大量实验表明，数据层水印溯源与计算层零知识聚合在复合威胁场景下具有互补保护效果；RLWE设计提供后量子安全属性。摘要未给出具体数据集、攻击配置、性能指标、对比方法或数值结果，因此实际增益和代价尚无法量化判断。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要性在于把来源认证水印与可验证隐私聚合统一到联邦学习工作流中，形成数据可信性与更新可信性的联合保障。其创新主张具备合理性，但需进一步审查PAM可信假设、密钥管理与撤销机制、水印对任务精度和鲁棒性的影响、零知识证明的通信计算开销，以及面对客户端串谋和基础设施元数据被伪造时的安全性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to multi-level attacks. However, existing methods address them separately, leaving FL exposed to data leakage, unauthorized reuse, and malicious gradient manipulation. In this work, we propose an FL framework that couples keyed context-provenance watermarking with verifiable lattice-based secure aggregation of Real-World Anchored Watermarking and Lattice-Based Zero-Knowledge Secure Aggregation. At the data layer, we propose a Kerckhoffs-compliant scheme that utilizes Physical Anchor Metadata (PAM) to ensure data provenance. PAM is defined as a context-provenance token derived from trusted infrastructure data (time, location, and server ID) and then subjected to a keyed HMAC-SHA-256 transformation to produce a watermark payload that cannot be generated without the client's secret key. We further design FMGAN, a GAN-based robust image watermarking framework that embeds this transformed payload using a feature fusion module and a Mamba-guided linear attention mechanism. At the computation layer, we adopt a lattice-based zero-knowledge secure aggregation (LZKSA) protocol that verifies key correctness, L2 norm bounds, and cosine similarity constraints over committed gradients without revealing private updates. The RLWE-based design guarantees post-quantum security. Extensive experiments validate the complementary protection of the two layers under composite attack scenarios. To our knowledge, no prior verification workflow has jointly evaluated both layers in a hybrid, end-to-end trustworthy FL framework.

</details>


### [26] [MATEE: Efficiently Bridging the Semantic Gap in TrustZone via Arm Pointer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20583)
*Shiqi Liu,Xiang Li,Jie Wang,Yongpeng Gao,Jiajin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文针对Arm TrustZone中普通世界与安全世界的信息语义鸿沟问题，提出MATEE系统。该系统利用Arm指针认证机制将请求与客户端应用身份绑定，并在访问敏感数据时校验身份，以阻止伪造请求和跨应用数据访问；实验显示其运行时开销仅为2.19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TrustZone将系统划分为安全世界和普通世界后，安全世界难以获得普通世界中请求发起者的可靠身份语义。现有受信应用在处理来自普通世界的数据请求时，可能仅依据可被操纵的参数判断归属，导致恶意客户端应用可伪造精心构造的请求，进而访问其他客户端应用关联的敏感数据。

Method: 作者首先系统化分类语义鸿沟漏洞，然后设计MATEE作为TEE侧防护机制。MATEE借助Arm Pointer Authentication，将每个请求与对应客户端应用的身份进行密码学绑定；当请求访问敏感数据时，受信环境验证该绑定以确认调用者身份。该设计按客户端应用隔离敏感数据，并宣称无需修改既有客户端应用和受信应用。

Result: 评估表明，MATEE能够防御文中定义的语义鸿沟漏洞及相关请求伪造行为，同时保持较低性能成本，报告的最小运行时开销为2.19%。

Conclusion: 该工作指出TrustZone跨世界调用中的身份语义缺失是重要攻击面，并通过基于硬件指针认证的请求身份绑定机制提供了兼容性较好的缓解方案。其核心贡献在于将客户端身份验证前移并纳入敏感数据访问控制，从而实现不同客户端应用数据的隔离。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) employ hardware-based isolation mechanisms to safeguard the confidentiality and integrity of sensitive code and data. One such prevalent implementation is Arm TrustZone, which partitions the system into the secure and normal (non-secure) worlds. However, this partitioning results in the secure world having very limited visibility into the operating information of the normal world, creating a semantic gap between these two worlds. Specifically, the secure world lacks an effective user identity authentication when receiving data requests from the normal world. Consequently, malicious Client Applications (CAs) in the normal world can deceive Trusted Applications (TAs) in the secure world by utilizing elaborate request parameters, compromising the sensitive data stored by other CAs. We systematically classify these Semantic Gap Vulnerabilities (SGVs) and propose a mate system for the TEE called MATEE to defend against SGVs. MATEE utilizes Arm Pointer Authentication (PA) to bind each request to the corresponding CA's identity and then verifies the identity when the CA accesses sensitive data, thereby preventing malicious request forgery. In particular, MATEE isolates sensitive data of different CAs without modifying existing CAs and TAs. Our evaluation demonstrates that MATEE successfully defends against SGVs with a minimal runtime overhead (2.19%).

</details>


### [27] [More Granular, Less Trust: Enforcing Intra-Process Isolation with Arm CCA in an Untrusted Management Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20584)
*Shiqi Liu,Zhouqi Jiang,Jie Wang,Wei Zhou,Kun Sun,Zhaohui Chen,Yulai Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CCAegis 基于 Arm CCA 实现进程内机密隔离，使敏感数据和操作同时免受操作系统及进程内对手的访问；在真实密码学工作负载上，性能开销为 1.01×至 1.43×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CVM 和细粒度隔离方案仍依赖操作系统管理隔离域，难以防御进程内攻击与操作系统和进程内对手的合谋，并导致较大的可信计算基。论文旨在构建更细粒度、低信任依赖的机密计算机制。

Method: CCAegis 扩展 Arm Confidential Compute Architecture，利用静态分析跟踪敏感数据流并识别相关函数；在函数调用和返回位置插入权限切换指令，通过 Granule Protection Table 调整内存访问权限，仅允许指定函数访问隔离数据。系统仅信任 Secure Monitor，由其配置 GPT 并管理安全域切换，从而缩小可信计算基。

Result: 作者在官方模拟器和真实开发板上实现并评估了 CCAegis。实验表明，该系统能够有效隔离敏感数据与操作；相较原始版本，在真实密码学工作负载上的性能为 1.01×至 1.43×，即额外开销较低。

Conclusion: CCAegis 证明了基于 Arm CCA 和 GPT 的进程内隔离可在较小可信计算基下提供更强的数据与操作保护，并具备较好的实际性能。然而，其安全性和开销仍可能依赖静态分析准确性、函数边界识别以及权限切换频率。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of confidential computing, security-sensitive applications are often deployed in confidential virtual machines (CVMs), which reduce reliance on third-party cloud providers. However, privilege attacks originating from the OS remain a significant threat in these environments. Existing finer-grained isolation schemes, such as SHELTER, provide process-level protection but are still vulnerable to intraprocess attacks and potential collusion between the OS and intra-process adversaries. Many current intra-process isolation techniques continue to depend on the OS to manage and enforce isolation domains, leading to a large Trusted Computing Base (TCB). This gap highlights the need for more granular, less trust-dependent confidential computing solutions. In this paper, we present CCAegis, a system that extends the Arm Confidential Compute Architecture (CCA) to enforce intra-process isolation of sensitive data and operations, safeguarding them from both intraprocess adversaries and the OS. We employ static analysis to track the flow of sensitive data and identify functions that handle such data. Permission-switching instructions are inserted at the function call and return points, adjusting permissions via the Granule Protection Table (GPT) to ensure that only designated functions can access the isolated data. Notably, CCAegis places trust solely in the Secure Monitor, which configures the GPTs and manages domain switching, thereby minimizing the TCB. We implemented CCAegis on both an official emulator and a real development board to assess its performance. Our experimental results show that CCAegis effectively isolates sensitive data and operations, with performance overheads ranging from 1.01x to 1.43x compared to the original version across real-world cryptographic workloads.

</details>


### [28] [ARQ: Agentic CodeQL Query Refinement for C/C++ Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20637)
*Chunyi Wang,Yunfei Ke,Junfeng Yang,Yun-Yun Tsai,Penghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ARQ：一个利用合成C/C++程序的实际执行结果作为证据、自动改进CodeQL检测查询的智能体框架。其目标是在不依赖人工标注数据、代码提交历史或特定漏洞模板的条件下，同时降低误报并减少漏报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的静态分析工具通过预定义规则匹配缺陷模式，但规则往往难以准确覆盖复杂的真实程序语义，因而会将安全代码误判为问题，或遗漏真实问题。现有查询改进方法通常依赖标注数据、历史修复提交或人工设计的模式，适用成本和泛化能力受限。

Method: ARQ首先合成C/C++测试程序，并通过程序执行确定其实际安全属性；再将执行结果与CodeQL查询的告警结果对照。若实际存在问题但查询未报告，则形成漏报反例；若程序安全却被查询报告，则形成误报反例。随后，框架以这些执行—查询不一致样本作为近似真值，驱动基于大语言模型的迭代式查询修改与验证流程，直至获得更准确的查询。实验中使用三种商用大语言模型，对12条官方CodeQL查询进行改进。

Result: 在Juliet v1.3和FormAI v2数据集上，ARQ改进后的查询相较原始查询可显著提高真阳性检出数量，最高增幅为119.8%；各项结果的精确率始终不低于98.0%。此外，ARQ修复了官方CodeQL查询仓库中3个长期未解决的问题，其中最长已开放27个月；改进后的查询还在libpng和zlib中发现了2个此前未知的缺陷。

Conclusion: ARQ证明了“以程序执行结果校验静态查询结论”的方法能够为查询优化提供有效监督信号。该框架以较少外部先验自动发现并修复查询的语义盲区，在维持高精确率的同时提升检出能力，表明其具有用于静态分析规则持续维护和实际软件审计的潜力。

Abstract: Static analyzers have been widely adopted for vulnerability detection in C/C++ programs. Query-based static analyzers (e.g., CodeQL) encode vulnerable code patterns in detection queries and match them against source code. However, existing queries still suffer from false positives (FPs, incorrectly flagging benign code as vulnerable) and false negatives (FNs, missing real vulnerabilities). We present ARQ, an agentic framework that automatically refines C/C++ CodeQL queries using execution-grounded evidence from synthesized C/C++ programs. Our key insight is that a synthesized program exposes a query's weakness whenever its execution disagrees with the query's verdict. If the program is genuinely vulnerable but the query stays silent, the query has an FN weakness; if the program is safe but the query fires anyway, it has an FP weakness. ARQ then runs an LLM-based refinement loop that repairs the query using these disagreements as ground truth. Unlike previous query refining methods, ARQ requires no labeled datasets, no commit history, and no vulnerability-specific templates. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ARQ by refining 12 official CodeQL queries using three commercial LLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, and Gemini-3.5-flash). We compare both ARQ-refined and original CodeQL queries on the Juliet v1.3 and FormAI v2 datasets and show that ARQ-refined queries detect substantially more true positives, by up to 119.8\%, with a Precision of at least 98.0\% throughout. ARQ successfully fixed three unresolved GitHub issues raised in the official CodeQL query repository that had remained open for as long as \textit{27 months}. The refined queries also exposed two previously undiscovered bugs in the real-world libraries libpng and zlib.

</details>


### [29] [The Claws in Plain Sight: Unauthorized Context Disclosure through LLM Agent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20658)
*Ben Dong,Zhonghao Guo,Tianyi Lu,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“Claw in Plain Sight”攻击：任务相邻内容通过制造“操作或流程必需”的权威压力，诱导智能体在工具调用参数中夹带本不应为当前目的或目标传输的受保护上下文信息。受控实验显示，仅靠提示词隐私策略无法稳定阻止此类越权生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可访问用户档案、对话历史、检索文档及先前工具结果，但“可访问”不等于“可为任意用途或目的地传输”。现有系统常缺少对工具调用参数中信息流目的与接收方的细粒度约束，因此需要检验模型是否会在看似合法的任务参数构造中泄露敏感属性。

Method: 作者构建合成基准，将6个权威压力等级、4个隐私策略等级与5种DeepSeek和Claude模型配置交叉组合，共评估120次工具调用。攻击文本将受保护属性描述为完成相邻任务所必需的内容；评测在上下文到工具参数生成边界捕获模型拟议参数，并统计会话级披露率。所有用户档案均为合成数据，参数仅在本地记录。

Result: 不同模型及压力—策略组合下，会话级披露率介于20.8%至75.0%。更强的隐私指令能够降低总体披露水平，但无法在所有模型和配置中持续消除披露。这表明提示词层面的隐私政策缺乏跨模型、可移植且可靠的执行边界。实验衡量的是违规参数生成，而非真实网络传输、已执行的外泄或部署用户数据泄露。

Conclusion: 工具调用安全不能仅依赖模型遵循自然语言隐私指令。论文主张在执行前对生成参数实施面向“使用目的”和“接收目的地”的检查与强制策略，以阻止上下文信息被不当嵌入原本有效的工具调用中。

Abstract: LLM agents routinely construct tool-call arguments from user profiles, conversation history, retrieved documents, and prior tool results. However, legitimate access to contextual information does not imply authorization to transmit that information for every purpose or destination. We present Claw in Plain Sight, an authority- pressure attack in which task-adjacent content frames protected attributes as operationally or procedurally required, causing a model to include them in otherwise valid generated arguments. We evaluate Claw in Plain Sight using a controlled synthetic benchmark that crosses six pressure levels with four privacy-policy levels across five DeepSeek and Claude model configurations, producing 120 calls. Across the complete pressure-policy matrix, session-level disclosure rates range from 20.8% to 75.0% among the tested models. Stronger privacy instructions reduce aggregate disclosure but do not eliminate it consistently across models, showing that prompt-level policies do not provide a portable enforcement boundary. Our experiments use only synthetic profiles and capture proposed arguments locally; they measure policy-violating generation at the context-to-argument boundary, not completed network exfiltration or leakage from deployed users. These findings motivate purpose- and destination-aware inspection of generated tool arguments before execution.

</details>


### [30] [Bootstrapping Mutual Attestation with Kleene's Second Recursion Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20671)
*Takuma Imamura*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需中心可信运营方、可信第三方或外部参考值的互认证参考值自举方法。核心思想是将节点间参考值的循环依赖形式化为互递归不动点方程，并借助 Kleene 第二递归定理构造可相互重建源码的节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互认证要求每个节点持有对等节点的预期代码度量值。若直接把这些参考值嵌入各节点代码，则参考值会随代码变化而变化，形成无限回归。现有方案通常依赖可信第三方、外部配置或特定硬件/架构的度量机制，限制了去中心化和可移植性。

Method: 作者把参考值自举建模为一组互相依赖的固定点方程，利用 Kleene 第二递归定理生成相互引用的程序。每个节点仅凭内置数据即可重建所有对等节点的精确源码：对于直接度量源码的场景，直接对重建源码执行度量函数；对于度量构建产物的场景，先以可复现方式从重建源码构建镜像，再计算其参考度量。原型包括用于 Python 的 PyReflect，以及用于 Nix 构建描述的 NixReflect。

Result: PyReflect 被用于实现基于 TPM 的互认证概念验证；NixReflect 被用于双 AWS Nitro Enclave 概念验证，其中两个节点可仅依靠内置数据复现彼此的参考 PCR 值。该方案可兼容现有认证栈，无需修改底层认证流程。

Conclusion: 该工作以递归论为基础解决了互认证中的参考值无限回归问题，证明可在不依赖可信第三方和外部参考值的条件下实现架构无关的参考值自举。其实际适用性仍依赖于源码重建的精确性、构建过程的可复现性，以及部署环境中度量对象与构建链的一致性。

Abstract: Mutual attestation among nodes with no central trusted operator requires each node to hold reference values (expected code measurements) for its peers. The naïve approach of mutually embedding these reference values in the nodes' code leads to an infinite regress. We call the problem of resolving this infinite regress the reference-value bootstrapping problem for mutual attestation. Existing solutions avoid this regress by relying on a trusted third party (TTP), externally supplied reference values, or architecture-specific measurement mechanisms. We instead express the bootstrapping problem as a system of mutual fixed-point equations and solve it by Kleene's second recursion theorem. The construction produces nodes that mutually reference one another's code and reconstruct every peer's exact source from built-in data alone. When a deployed source file is measured directly, as with a Python script, a node obtains the peer's reference value by applying the measurement function directly to the reconstructed source. When a built image is measured, as with AWS Nitro Enclaves, a node instead reproducibly rebuilds the peer's image from the reconstructed source and derives its reference measurement. For the first case, we develop PyReflect, a Python transpiler, and use it to implement a TPM mutual-attestation PoC. For the second, we develop NixReflect, a Nix transpiler, and use it in a PoC in which two Nitro Enclaves reproduce each other's reference PCRs from built-in data alone. Our solution is architecture-independent, requires neither a TTP nor externally supplied reference values, and works with existing attestation stacks unchanged.

</details>


### [31] [The Rising Cost of Trust: Practitioners' Trust Signals, Controls, and Responses in the Software Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20675)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Siri Paidipalli,Laurie Williams,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究通过访谈38名产业界与开源从业者，发现软件供应链中的信任正在减弱，并导致验证、治理与退出等成本上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链复杂度增加，人工智能生成依赖的质量等问题进一步改变风险格局；但现有研究缺少对从业者实际信任状况及其应对行为的经验基线。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈，覆盖产业界和开源从业者；重点考察受访者已部署的控制措施这一“揭示偏好”，而非主观态度；随后以社会科学中的既有信任概念为理论基础进行主题分析。

Result: 从业者普遍感知到信任侵蚀，并为此不断累积控制措施，形成更高的操作与管理成本。主要应对路径包括：自动化验证、将信任决策委托给“信任守护者”、以及在极端情况下考虑退出相关供应链。

Conclusion: 信任是理解软件供应链运行与治理的关键分析框架。研究提出“信任守护者”“系统信任”“信号”等概念，可用于设计未来干预措施，在保障可靠性的同时维持适当而非过度的信任成本。

Abstract: The software supply chain is becoming more complex, and AI is reshaping its threat landscape, e.g., raising concerns about the quality of AI-generated dependencies. Seen through the lens of trust, the stakes of eroding trust in the software supply chain are high, yet we lack an empirical baseline on practitioners' trust. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in taking informed actions as trust in the software supply chain evolves, through an interview study with 38 practitioners. We conducted semi-structured interviews with industry and open-source practitioners, focusing on their revealed preferences (the controls they adopted) rather than their stated attitudes, and analyzed the data using thematic analysis grounded in established trust concepts from the social sciences. We find that trust is eroding, which is becoming costly: aware practitioners are accumulating controls. To cope with the rising cost of trust, practitioners automate verification, delegate trust decisions to guardians, or consider exiting the software supply chain entirely. Understanding software supply chain dynamics through the lens of trust provides the vocabulary and concepts (e.g., guardians of trust, system trust, signals) to shape future interventions for a well-functioning supply chain with appropriate levels of trust.

</details>


### [32] [The Software Supply Chain as a Market for Lemons: A Multivocal Review of Trust Signal Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20678)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Christian Kästner,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究通过多声部综述指出，开源依赖评估中常用的星标、下载量和贡献者活跃度等低成本信任信号，正同时受到恶意操纵、难以与正常行为区分的刷量手法，以及合法AI工具带来的非恶意膨胀影响，因此不足以稳定反映依赖项的真实可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者通常无法逐一审查依赖代码，因而以公开指标替代直接验证。既有研究多聚焦单一指标被操纵的问题，而该研究旨在系统梳理各类依赖采纳信号失效的整体图景，以及生态系统对此的实际应对程度。

Method: 作者对252个Google Search来源和870个Reddit讨论串进行多声部文献综述与编码分析，归纳信任信号失效的驱动因素，并区分建议性回应与已发生的实际行为改变。

Result: 分析发现，低成本信任信号在三种力量共同作用下失效：恶意操纵、与合法行为难以区分的游戏化技术、以及AI工具导致的非恶意指标通胀。54.6%的Google来源仅提供实践建议而未报告实际行动。常见方案是改用其他低成本指标或聚合多个指标，但这些替代方案同样可被操纵；对于AI导致的通胀，两类语料中均缺少已实施行为变化的证据。

Conclusion: 当伪造信号的成本低于获得信号的成本时，高质量与低质量依赖将趋于不可区分，形成类似“柠檬市场”的信息失真。研究认为，依赖个人实践者核验公开指标不可持续；应将加密证明等成本更高、可验证性更强的信号设为默认且强制性的生态机制，而非仅供少数自愿采用。

Abstract: Practitioners evaluating open-source dependencies rely on cheap trust signals, e.g., stars, download counts, and contributor activity, as substitutes for direct code inspection, assuming those signals reflect genuine trustworthiness. Prior work has documented individual signal gaming, but the landscape of collapses across all dependency-adoption signals, as well as the ecosystem's response, remains unexplored. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in understanding the reliability of dependency adoption trust signals, such as download counts and contributor activity, by conducting a multivocal review of 252 Google Search sources and 870 Reddit threads. After coding the corpora, we find that cheap trust signals collapse under three simultaneous forces: adversarial manipulation, gaming techniques indistinguishable from legitimate behavior, and non-adversarial AI-driven inflation. The documented responses are more advice than actual action: 54.6% of Google Search sources contain advice on what practitioners should do, with no actual action taken. Responses proposed substituting one cheap signal for another or aggregating multiple signals, which are now also gameable. Non-adversarial inflation, i.e., degradation caused by the emergence of legitimate AI tooling, lacks documented actual behavior change in either corpus. The gap between known remedy and actual practice points toward a market for lemons: when faking signals costs less than earning them, good and bad dependencies become indistinguishable. Relying on individual practitioners to verify the cheap signals is not sustainable. Costlier signals, such as cryptographic attestation, should be made mandatory so that they become the default for all, not a voluntary choice for the few.

</details>


### [33] [Enabling Threshold Custody for the Lightning Network with Nested Threshold Multi-Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20705)
*Paul Gerhart,Nadav Kohen,Jesse Posner,Matias Furszyfer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Iceberg，一种嵌套阈值MuSig2签名方案，使闪电网络通道的一方可由t-of-n阈值群组共同控制，同时对对手方保持标准MuSig2参与者的外部接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪电网络通道通常依赖单个在线私钥，存在单点失陷风险。尽管链上场景已可采用阈值签名保护密钥，但现有方案难以在不改变既有MuSig2随机数交换与消息流程的条件下，将通道参与方内部阈值化。

Method: 作者形式化“嵌套阈值多重签名”这一密码学原语，用于在多重签名协议的一名参与者内部嵌入阈值签名；据此构造Iceberg，实现嵌套阈值MuSig2。论文进一步给出安全性证明，将原型集成至生产级闪电网络节点，并进行性能基准测试。

Result: Iceberg允许通道一侧以t-of-n群组方式签名，而无需修改比特币协议、闪电网络协议或通道对手方。实验显示，在可容忍一名成员失陷的阈值配置下，其支付吞吐量仍超过未修改单密钥端点的93%，表明额外开销较小。

Conclusion: Iceberg为闪电网络提供了可单边部署的阈值托管能力，在维持现有协议兼容性和较高吞吐量的同时，显著缓解了通道端点单在线密钥带来的单点风险。

Abstract: The Bitcoin Lightning Network secures hundreds of millions of dollars, yet channel endpoints rely on vulnerable single online keys. Although threshold signatures are routinely used to protect on-chain Bitcoin, no practical deployment has been possible for Lightning channels. This is because thresholdizing a Lightning party requires nesting a threshold signature scheme inside of an established two-party MuSig2 protocol without altering its nonce exchange or message flow.
  In this work, we resolve this limitation by formalizing nested threshold multi-signatures, a new cryptographic primitive for thresholdizing one participant inside a multi-signature protocol. As an instance of this primitive, we present Iceberg, the first construction for nested threshold MuSig2 signatures.
